Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising approach for tabular data, yet its efficacy under extreme label scarcity and test-time missingness remains under-explored. In this paper, we evaluate a mask-and-recover SSL pretraining objective against training from scratch and classical baselines across 14 diverse classification tasks. First, while SSL outperforms training from scratch on average and remains competitive with state-of-the-art tree ensembles (achieving ~0.8954 AUC vs. Random Forest's 0.9015 at 10% labels), the SSL-vs-scratch gains exhibit high inter-task variance and lack significance (p = 0.626 at both 5% and 10% labels). Second, contrary to the hypothesis that missing-value imputation objectives universally benefit datasets with native missingness, SSL yields the most reliable improvements on clean datasets, while frequently degrading performance on datasets with high inherent missingness. Third, despite this training variance, SSL-pretrained models achieve a higher average AUC than scratch-trained models under both test-time missingness completely at random (MCAR) injection (+0.0245 AUC, positive on 11 of 14 tasks) and structured missingness shifts (MNAR, +0.0418 AUC, positive on 8 of 14 tasks), though neither difference remains statistically significant after Holm-Bonferroni correction for multiple comparisons (adjusted p = 0.118 and p = 0.518, respectively). Fourth, comparing our mask-and-recover objective against three established tabular SSL baselines (VIME, SCARF, SubTab) under an identical encoder architecture, we find no significant difference from any of them (adjusted p = 0.459, p = 1.000, p = 1.000), indicating our findings reflect general properties of tabular SSL rather than idiosyncrasies of one particular pretext task.

</details>


### [200] [Equivariant Covariance Tensors: Guaranteed SPD Uncertainty for Tensor-Valued Geometric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24386)
*Ruihan Liu,Yu Ji,Jianbo Yu,Shifu Yan,Qingchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种E(3)-等变不确定性量化框架，用于对称秩-2张量预测，通过将协方差分解为不可约表示，并利用矩阵指数映射确保正定性，同时保持等变性；引入基于多变量拉普拉斯分布的对数欧几里得等变评分目标（LE-ESO），实现鲁棒优化与物理一致的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有E(3)-等变神经网络在点估计上表现优异，但缺乏严格的置信度度量，尤其在对称秩-2张量预测中，完整不确定性需由6×6协方差矩阵表示，传统方法难以保证正定性和旋转对称性，因此亟需一种兼顾等变性与不确定性建模的新方法。

Method: 将协方差分解为不可约表示 $\mathrm{Sym}^2(ρ_c) \cong 2\times(l=0) \oplus 2\times(l=2) \oplus 1\times(l=4)$，通过从平坦李代数 $\mathfrak{sym}(6)$ 到曲面对称正定（SPD）流形的矩阵指数映射，严格保证协方差正定性并维持等变性；设计了基于多变量拉普拉斯分布的对数欧几里得等变评分目标（LE-ESO），提升对重尾误差的鲁棒性与优化稳定性。

Result: 在ModelNet40惯性张量和Materials Project介电张量数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能上具有竞争力，且能提供物理上一致、对称性保持的不确定性估计，具备良好的风险感知与分布外（OOD）敏感性。

Conclusion: 所提出的E(3)-等变不确定性量化框架成功实现了对称秩-2张量预测中的全分布建模，兼具数学严谨性、物理合理性与实际有效性，为几何深度学习中的不确定性量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tensor-valued prediction is fundamental to geometric deep learning, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such outputs remains an open challenge. While E(3)-equivariant neural networks excel at point estimates, they lack rigorous confidence measures. We focus on symmetric rank-2 tensor prediction, where the target has six Kelvin--Mandel coordinates and full uncertainty is represented by a $6\times6$ covariance matrix. We introduce a framework for E(3)-equivariant UQ, modeling the full predictive distribution where both mean and covariance preserve rotational symmetry. Our approach decomposes the covariance into irreducible representations $\mathrm{Sym}^2(ρ_c) \cong 2\times(l=0) \oplus 2\times(l=2) \oplus 1\times(l=4)$. By mapping from the flat Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sym}(6)$ to the curved SPD manifold via matrix exponentiation, we strictly ensure positive-definite covariances while maintaining exact equivariance. Furthermore, we formulate a Log-Euclidean Equivariant Scoring Objective (LE-ESO)---a robust surrogate loss based on the Multivariate Laplace distribution---providing robustness to heavy-tailed errors and stable optimization. Validation on ModelNet40 inertia tensors and Materials Project dielectric tensors demonstrates that our method achieves competitive performance and provides physically consistent, symmetry-preserving uncertainty estimates with useful risk and OOD sensitivity.

</details>


### [201] [Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24429)
*Shizhe Li,Hongshan Pu,Mengying Xie,Yi Xiang,Xiaowei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对通用域适应（UniDA）问题，提出了一个泛化误差上界，并基于此设计了新的JAUA算法，通过最小化卡方散度来对齐联合分布，同时提出渐进式伪标签方法为无标签目标样本分配伪标签。实验在六个公开图像数据集上验证了JAUA的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界应用中源域和目标域的标签空间可能存在差异，导致不仅存在样本分布偏移，还存在类别空间差异，传统无监督域适应方法无法有效处理此类问题，因此需要理论分析与新算法支持。

Method: 提出泛化误差上界，设计基于卡方散度的联合分布对齐方法（JAUA），并引入渐进式伪标签策略以提升目标域样本标注质量。

Result: 在六个公开图像数据集上的实验表明，JAUA算法在处理通用域适应问题上表现优越，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析和新算法设计，有效应对了通用域适应中的分布偏移与类别空间差异问题，验证了所提方法在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) has been widely concerned in the fields of machine learning, pattern recognition, and computer vision. Traditional UDA learning usually assumes that the label spaces of the source and target domains are exactly the same and only needs to solve the problem of sample distribution drift existing between two domains. However, in real world applications, the label spaces between two domains may be different. In this case, there are both sample distribution drift and class spatial difference between domains, namely Universal Domain Adaptation (UniDA) learning scenario. At present, existing works rarely offer theoretical analysis for universal domain adaptation. In this paper, we provide an upper bound of the generalization error for universal domain adaptation. According to the proposed generalization error bound, we propose a novel UniDA algorithm called Joint Distribution Alignment for Universal Domain Adaptation (JAUA), which aligns the joint distributions by minimizing the distribution discrepancy calculated by Chi-Square divergence. Furthermore, we propose a progressive pseudo-labeling method to assign the pseudo labels to unlabeled target samples. The experiment results on six public image datasets demonstrate the superiority of JAUA in handling the UniDA problem.

</details>


### [202] [Evaluating Deep Multivariate Imputation Models on Wearable Device Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24436)
*Skye Goodman,Roussel Desmond Nzoyem,Leandro Junges,Peter Kissack,Yasser Qureshi,Amberly Brigden,Jeff Clark,Nawid Keshtmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对可穿戴设备生理数据中的结构化缺失问题，提出了一种基于连续缺失段模板的评估与训练协议，通过保留特征间的共缺失结构，更真实地模拟实际缺失模式。实验表明，该协议能显著提升BRITS在严重缺失情况下的表现（MAE降低43%），并揭示不同模型在不同场景下的优劣：线性插值适用于缓慢变化特征的短缺失，扩展版BRITS在中度至重度缺失下表现更好，而SAITS虽MAE较高但更优地保持了真实分布。研究强调评估设计对模型排名的影响，呼吁采用更贴近现实的评估方法以推动未来多传感器可穿戴数据补全技术的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度补全方法如BRITS和SAITS在多模态生理数据上的评估受限于随机点剔除的缺失模拟方式，无法反映真实场景中特征间共缺失的结构性缺失模式，导致评估结果失真，影响模型真实性能判断。

Method: 从癫痫患者佩戴Garmin手表的数据中挖掘连续缺失段模板，按每特征缺失长度分位数分层，并注入为保留共缺失结构的块掩码；同时设计匹配的训练协议，使模型暴露于相似的缺失分布；进一步扩展BRITS引入时间日周期编码和昼夜节律谐波通道。

Result: 所提评估与训练协议使BRITS在严重缺失情况下的平均绝对误差（MAE）降低43%；不同模型表现各异：线性插值在慢变特征短缺失中最优，扩展BRITS在动态心率特征中度/重度缺失下表现更佳，SAITS虽MAE较高但更优地保持真实分布（以Jensen-Shannon距离衡量）。

Conclusion: 传统评估方法因忽略缺失结构而掩盖模型真实能力，本文提出的可迁移评估与训练协议为开发更可靠的多传感器可穿戴数据补全策略提供了关键步骤。

Abstract: Wearable device data enables continuous health monitoring, but suffers from structured missingness: features sharing a physical sensor drop out together. Deep imputation methods such as BRITS and SAITS have seen limited evaluation on multimodal physiological data under realistic missingness, and existing benchmarks use random-point holdout protocols that incorrectly assume missingness is independent across features and time. Using data from a person with epilepsy recorded on a Garmin smartwatch, we develop an evaluation protocol that mines contiguous missing-run templates from training data, stratifies them by per-feature gap-length quantiles, and injects them as block masks with preserved co-missingness structure. A matched training protocol exposing models to the same missingness distribution reduces BRITS's severe-gap MAE by 43%, demonstrating the potential benefit of the proposed evaluation and training protocol within this single-participant dataset. We further extend BRITS with time-of-day encoding and a circadian harmonic channel. No single model dominates: linear interpolation is optimal for slow-moving features over short gaps; extended BRITS achieves lower MAE on dynamic cardiac features in moderate and severe gaps; and SAITS better preserves the ground-truth distribution by Jensen-Shannon distance despite higher MAE. Ultimately, model rankings strongly depend on evaluation designs. By exposing how traditional evaluation methods obscure true model capabilities, our transferable protocol establishes critical steps towards developing better imputation strategies for future multi-sensor wearable datasets.

</details>


### [203] [WarpSAC: Towards the Pinnacle of Scalable Off-policy RL by Rethinking Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24479)
*Zihao Wu,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Huizhong Song,Pengyi Li,Yifu Yuan,Fei Ni,Jinyi Liu,Wei Wei,Jianrong Wang,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大规模并行模拟对离策略强化学习（RL）训练数据格局的影响，发现传统稳定化方法在不同数据规模下表现不一。提出WarpSAC，一种根据数据可用性自适应调整的离策略RL算法家族，包含针对数据受限（WarpSAC-L）和数据丰富（WarpSAC-A）场景的变体。实验表明，WarpSAC在多种环境中显著优于FlashSAC，提升了学习效率、成功率和部署速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行模拟改变了离策略强化学习的训练数据格局，使得原有为数据有限环境设计的稳定化技术不再适用，亟需根据数据可用性自适应调整算法设计。

Method: 通过控制实验分析不同稳定化技术在不同数据规模下的表现，基于发现提出WarpSAC算法，采用样本权重衰减机制，并设计两种适配不同数据环境的变体：WarpSAC-L（参数归一化开启，剪裁双Q）用于数据受限的CPU训练；WarpSAC-A（参数归一化关闭，单Q）用于数据丰富的GPU并行训练。

Result: WarpSAC在九个CPU环境上相比FlashSAC提升4.5%的归一化得分-步数AUC，十四个GPU环境提升23.1%；在UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1任务中成功率从19.8%提升至96.4%；在MuJoCo Playground上平均归一化墙时长AUC提升19.1%；在真实世界部署中比FlashSAC快36.4%。

Conclusion: 可扩展的离策略强化学习应根据可用数据规模动态调整其稳定化策略，WarpSAC通过引入数据域感知机制，实现了更高效、鲁棒的训练与部署。

Abstract: Massively parallel simulation changes the data regime in which off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is trained, challenging stabilizers designed for data-limited replay. Through controlled experiments across eight benchmark families, we show that these stabilizers are data-regime-dependent: parameter normalization helps with narrow replay coverage but restricts value fitting when data are abundant, while clipped double-Q can be relaxed in high-throughput manipulation. Age-biased replay weighting improves learning efficiency across regimes, especially with limited network capacity.
  Based on these findings, we propose WarpSAC, a regime-aware family of off-policy RL algorithms. WarpSAC uses Sample Weight Decay for efficient exploitation and provides two variants: WarpSAC-L (Norm ON, clipped double-Q) for data-limited CPU-scale training, and WarpSAC-A (Norm OFF, single-Q) for data-abundant GPU-parallel training. WarpSAC improves normalized score--step AUC over FlashSAC by 4.5% across nine CPU-scale environments and 23.1% across fourteen GPU-parallel environments. It increases UnitreeG1TransportBox-v1 success rate from 19.8% to 96.4%, improves mean normalized wall-time AUC on MuJoCo Playground by 19.1%, and achieves 36.4% faster sim-to-real deployment on Unitree G1 than FlashSAC. These results show that scalable off-policy RL should adapt its stabilizers to the available data regime.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Static Interpretability: Anticipating Post-SFT Mechanisms from Pre-SFT Parameters for Better Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24482)
*Hang Chen,Jiaying Zhu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种前瞻性的机制定位框架，通过预训练模型参数和目标数据集预测微调后的可解释性状态，克服传统方法因回溯性导致的偏差问题。利用泰勒展开建模参数演化，设计神经元与组件双粒度定位流程，在多种规模模型上验证了其在指导高效微调中的优越性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机制可解释性方法依赖于微调后模型，但对新任务而言，初始识别的关键神经元与最终模型中的关键机制差异大，导致误导性结论并干扰监督微调（SFT）。因此需要一种能提前准确预测任务关键机制的方法。

Method: 将SFT视为连续参数演化过程，基于泰勒展开建立预训练模型动态梯度与微调后机制目标之间的理论桥梁；设计神经元级与组件级双粒度定位流水线，仅使用预训练参数和目标数据进行前向预测。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升SFT指导效果，在不同模型规模下均具备强鲁棒性和时间可扩展性，成功实现对微调后关键机制的精准预测。

Conclusion: 本工作突破传统可解释性无法在训练前识别任务关键机制的根本局限，开创了以预测为导向的可解释性新范式，实现了机制可解释性与目标优化的深度融合。

Abstract: Mechanistic Localization bridges mechanistic interpretability and post-training optimization by isolating critical parameters via interpretative approaches and then guiding parameter-efficient Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) in a ``locating-then-tuning'' paradigm. However, due to the retrospective nature of mechanistic interpretability, directly interpreting pre-SFT models introduces misleading conclusions. Specifically for novel tasks, initially identified neurons differ drastically from those governing the final model, introducing biases that actively disrupt SFT. To address this, we propose a forward-looking localization framework that accurately estimates the post-SFT interpretability state using only pre-SFT parameters and the target dataset. Theoretically, we model SFT as a continuous parameter evolution, leveraging Taylor expansion to rigorously bridge the post-tuning mechanistic objective with the pre-SFT model's dynamic gradients. Practically, we design dual-granularity (neuron- and component-level) localization pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach not only provides superior SFT guidance but also exhibits robust performance and temporal scalability across increasing model sizes. This work transcends the fundamental limitation of traditional interpretability-its inability to identify task-critical mechanisms before they are trained-pioneering a predictive frontier that unites mechanistic interpretability with targeted optimization.

</details>


### [205] [Where Entropy Is Measured Matters: Policy Geometry in Bounded Continuous-Control PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24488)
*Yiyang He,Zhichun Zhou,Ziwei Wang,Tao Xue,Haolin Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在连续控制策略中，熵的测量位置如何影响PPO算法学习到的策略几何结构。实验表明，使用截断高斯分布时，89.07%的动作接近边界；即使方差为零，仍有83.83%的动作靠近边界，且82.12%的状态条件均值超出可执行区间。使用tanh映射也无法消除高方差区域。比较潜变量熵H(u)与执行动作熵H(a)发现，前者对均值梯度为零，后者通过雅可比矩阵引入向内的均值梯度。三种不同种子下，边界附近占据率分别为71.42%、29.76%和18.83%，对应于潜变量熵、无熵和执行动作熵。在狗站立任务中，结果一致，且该现象在共享状态评估和不同边界裕度下仍存在。直接施加均值惩罚可达到甚至超过执行熵带来的中心化效果，说明内部均值并非仅由执行熵产生。然而，相同的均值几何结构可伴随显著不同的方差和回报。因此，熵的测量空间是均值-方差耦合的设计选择，仅凭任务回报无法刻画有界策略的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究熵的测量位置对连续控制策略中策略几何结构的影响，特别是其对有界动作空间中策略行为的影响，以揭示当前方法在优化过程中可能存在的偏差。

Method: 采用多肌肉MyoLeg和狗站立任务进行实验，对比不同熵测量方式（潜变量熵H(u)、无熵、执行动作熵H(a)）下的策略表现；分析动作分布与状态条件均值的边界接近程度；利用独立的CleanRL实现验证结果一致性；测试不同边界裕度与共享状态设置下的稳定性。

Result: 在相同状态下，即使方差为零，仍有大量动作接近边界，且状态条件均值也常位于不可执行区间外；执行动作熵相比其他熵测量方式能更有效地将策略集中于中间区域；直接均值惩罚可实现类似中心化效果；但均值几何结构与方差及回报解耦，说明熵测量空间是耦合的均值-方差设计选择。

Conclusion: 熵的测量位置直接影响策略的几何结构，尤其在有界动作空间中。仅依赖任务回报无法全面描述策略形态，必须考虑熵的测量方式及其对均值与方差的联合影响。

Abstract: Many continuous-control policies are optimized as unbounded Gaussians and then mapped into bounded actions. We show that where entropy is measured changes the policy geometry learned by proximal policy optimization (PPO). In an 80-muscle MyoLeg task, a clipped Gaussian executes 89.07% of actions within 5% of a bound. A same-state decomposition shows that this is not due to variance alone: setting variance to zero still leaves 83.83% of actions near a bound, while 82.12% of state-conditioned means lie outside the executable interval. Replacing clipping with a tanh map does not remove the high-variance regime. For latent Gaussian entropy H(u), the entropy loss has zero gradient with respect to the mean and a constant variance-increasing gradient. For executed-action entropy H(a), the transform Jacobian adds an inward gradient on the mean. Across three matched MyoLeg seeds, near-boundary occupancy is 71.42%, 29.76%, and 18.83% under latent entropy, no entropy, and executed-action entropy. A 38-dimensional Dog-Stand replication with an independent CleanRL-based PPO implementation reproduces the ordering in mean geometry, which also survives shared-state evaluation and boundary margins from 1% to 10%. Direct mean penalties can match or exceed the centering produced by H(a), showing that interior means are not unique to executed entropy. However, matched mean geometry can coexist with substantially different variance and return. Entropy measurement space is therefore a coupled mean-variance design choice, and task return alone does not characterize bounded-policy geometry.

</details>


### [206] [When Do Supervised UQ Ensembles Improve LLM Hallucination Detection? A Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24492)
*Mohit Singh Chauhan,Vipin Gyanchandani,Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种监督式集成框架，通过在小规模领域特定数据集上训练分类器来融合多种基于不确定性量化（UQ）的评分器输出，实现无需检索、工具或参考文档的幻觉检测。在四种LLM、九个数据集和三种生成模式下，系统评估了该方法在样本效率、领域内数据迁移和生成模式依赖性方面的鲁棒性。结果表明，监督式集成在32个设置中的30个中优于最佳单一评分器，且仅需100个标注样本即可获得显著提升；在分布偏移下的领域内迁移任务中，28个设置中有23个仍优于非集成评分器。基于采样的黑盒集成几乎与全集成效果相当，而单次生成的白盒集成增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不确定性量化（UQ）的方法虽可用于闭卷场景下的幻觉检测，但对多UQ信号融合的集成方法的鲁棒性缺乏充分实证研究。尤其在实际应用中，如何高效利用少量标注数据构建稳健的幻觉检测系统仍不明确。

Method: 提出一种监督式集成框架：在小规模、领域特定的标注数据集上训练一个分类器，以融合来自不同UQ评分器的输出；该模型在无外部信息支持的情况下应用于新样本的幻觉分类，涵盖多种生成任务类型。

Result: 监督式集成在32个实验设置中，在30个中超越最佳单一评分器，且仅需100个标注样本即可见效；在28个迁移任务中，23个仍优于非集成方法；采样型黑盒集成表现接近全集成，而白盒集成提升有限。

Conclusion: 监督式集成能够有效融合多种不确定性信号，在多样本、多任务、多模型场景下表现出优异的鲁棒性和样本效率，是当前闭卷幻觉检测中极具前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods are widely used for hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) in closed-book settings where ground-truth evidence is unavailable at inference time. Prior work has proposed combining UQ signals via learned ensembles, but empirical investigations into the robustness of these ensembles are limited. We study a supervised ensembling framework that trains a classifier over heterogeneous UQ-based scorer outputs on a small, domain-specific dataset of labeled LLM responses, then applies it to out-of-sample hallucination classification without retrieval, tools, or reference documents. Across four LLMs, nine datasets, and three generation regimes (short-form QA, long-form generation, and code generation), we provide a systematic robustness analysis along three axes: sample efficiency, in-domain dataset transfer, and generation regime dependence. We find that supervised ensembles outperform the best individual scorer in 30 of 32 settings, with gains realized from as few as 100 labeled instances. Ensembles retain most of their advantage in cases of in-domain transfer under distribution shift, outperforming the best non-ensemble scorer in 23 of 28 transfer settings. Sampling-based black-box ensembles are nearly as effective as full ensembles, while single-generation white-box ensembles offer limited benefit.

</details>


### [207] [It depends: Incorporating correlations for joint aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties of high-dimensional output spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24518)
*Leonhard F. Feiner,Manuel Nickel,Martin Menten,Laurin Lux,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis,Raphael Rehms,Johannes Paetzold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的不确定性量化方法，通过低秩加对角协方差结构联合建模高维回归任务中的认知不确定性（epistemic）和随机不确定性（aleatoric），实现高效且可扩展的联合不确定性表示。该方法支持采样与似然评估等下游分析，并在图像修复、着色、光流和深度估计任务中表现出优越的不确定性估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维输出场景中，仅建模单一类型的不确定性会限制预测的透明性与鲁棒性；联合建模认知不确定性和随机不确定性有助于更真实地反映数据噪声与模型知识盲区，提升深度学习系统的可靠性。

Method: 采用低秩加对角协方差结构近似联合不确定性分布，构建统一的二阶概率模型，显式融合两类不确定性，并引入稳定化训练策略以提升效率与收敛性。

Result: 在图像修复、着色、光流和深度估计等多个任务上，所提方法显著优于现有基准，在不确定性估计精度与计算效率方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了高维回归中双重不确定性的一体化建模，为可靠、可解释的深度学习预测提供了有效框架，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) plays a vital role in enhancing the reliability of deep learning model predictions, especially in scenarios with high-dimensional output spaces. This paper addresses the dual nature of uncertainty -- aleatoric and epistemic -- focusing on their joint integration in high-dimensional regression tasks. For example, in applications like medical image segmentation or restoration, aleatoric uncertainty captures inherent data noise, while epistemic uncertainty quantifies the model's confidence in unfamiliar conditions. Modeling both jointly enables more reliable predictions by reflecting both unavoidable variability and knowledge gaps, whereas modeling only one limits transparency and robustness. We propose a novel approach that approximates the resulting joint uncertainty using a low-rank plus diagonal covariance structure, capturing essential output correlations while avoiding the computational burdens of full covariance matrices. Unlike prior work, our method explicitly combines aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties into a unified second-order distribution that supports robust downstream analyses like sampling and log-likelihood evaluation. We further introduce stabilization strategies for efficient training and inference, achieving superior UQ in the tasks of image inpainting, colorization, optical flow, and depth estimation.

</details>


### [208] [From Numerical Simulators of PDEs to Neural Emulators and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24547)
*Felix Koehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了神经网络代理模型与传统数值求解器在求解偏微分方程（PDEs）中的相似性，提出二者在架构、误差分析和设计思想上存在深层共通性。通过分离求解器在训练流程中的多重角色，并运用模式傅里叶分析建立统一语言，论文提出了三个贡献：(1) APEBench基准测试套件，用于评估自回归神经代理模型；(2) 研究未收敛求解器对代理训练的影响；(3) 分析数值误差与架构归纳偏置对神经代理性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器在求解偏微分方程时计算成本高，限制了快速或多次查询的应用场景。虽然神经代理模型能显著加速，但其作为黑箱方法缺乏可解释性，难以与传统方法融合。本文旨在揭示神经架构与经典离散化方法之间的本质相似性，推动两者之间的知识互鉴。

Method: 通过分解求解器在神经代理训练中的多重角色，采用模式傅里叶分析作为统一框架，同时解析求解器误差、网络架构的归纳偏置以及训练目标之间的关系。利用JAX中的可微伪谱求解器构建高效仿真环境，支持大规模实验验证。

Result: 成功构建了APEBench基准平台，揭示了未收敛求解器对训练过程的显著影响，并证明了数值误差与架构设计共同决定神经代理性能。研究结果表明，神经模型并非完全脱离物理规律，而是可被系统性理解与优化的工具。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型与传统数值方法在结构与误差行为上具有内在一致性，通过统一的分析视角，可以实现更高效、可解释且可信赖的替代方案。这为未来融合物理先验与深度学习提供了理论基础与实践路径。

Abstract: Simulation is central to modern engineering and science, but the cost of numerical solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a bottleneck whenever fast or many-query evaluations are required. Neural emulators trained on solver-generated data promise significant speedups, yet they are usually framed as opaque alternatives to the very methods that produce their training signal. This thesis argues the two paradigms are more alike than different: neural architectures mirror classical discretizations, their errors are amenable to the same spectral analysis, and insight flows profitably in both directions. We approach the relationship by disentangling the multiple roles a solver plays in the emulator learning pipeline. Mode-wise Fourier analysis then provides a common language in which solver errors, architectural inductive biases, and training objectives can all be read off simultaneously. Taken together, this allows synthesizing three contributions. (1) APEBench, a comprehensive benchmarking suite for autoregressive neural emulators of PDEs that uses fast differentiable pseudo-spectral solvers in JAX. (2) Progressively Refined Differentiable Physics, an investigation of the effect of unconverged solvers on surrogate training. (3) Neural Emulator Superiority, an analysis of the influence of numerical errors and architectural inductive biases.

</details>


### [209] [Persistent Cross Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24549)
*Sijin Yeom,Jae-Hun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的持久交叉熵（PCE）方法，通过结合相似性函数和持久性加权来定义诱导概率，从而在不同事件空间的持久图之间建立联系。该方法解决了传统交叉熵在持久图上无法自然定义的问题，并证明了其稳定性和有效性。数值实验表明，PCE能够区分具有相同持久熵的图、分离动态系统的因果方向，并可作为知识蒸馏中的方向性拓扑损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久熵的交叉熵难以在不同事件空间的持久图间定义，因为两个持久图通常具有不同的事件空间。因此需要一种方法来桥接这些事件空间，使得交叉熵可以被合理扩展。

Method: 通过引入相似性函数与持久性加权，构建诱导概率分布，将一个持久图的信息映射到另一个持久图的事件空间中，并将未解释的概率质量分配给未解释的事件，从而定义持久交叉熵（PCE）。

Result: PCE具备良好的稳定性，能够在多个实验中有效区分具有相同持久熵的图，识别动态系统中的因果方向，并成功应用于知识蒸馏中的方向性拓扑损失。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将交叉熵推广至持久图，提出了持久交叉熵（PCE），并验证了其在拓扑数据分析中的实用性与理论优势。

Abstract: Persistent entropy is the Shannon entropy of a persistence-based probability measure defined on a persistence diagram. However, its cross-entropy version is not naturally defined because two persistence diagrams generally have different event spaces. To bridge these event spaces, we combine a similarity function with persistence weighting to define an induced probability. The induced probability reflects information from one diagram on the event space of the other diagram and assigns unexplained probability mass to the unexplained event. Using the induced probability, we extend cross entropy to persistence diagrams, called persistent cross entropy (PCE). We establish the main properties of both the induced probability and PCE and prove stability theorems for both. Through three numerical studies, we show that PCE distinguishes diagrams with the same persistent entropy, separates causal directions in dynamical systems without constructing a joint persistent diagram, and can be used as a directional topology loss for knowledge distillation.

</details>


### [210] [FraudBench: Protocol-Sensitive Benchmarking of Adversarial Robustness for Financial Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24551)
*Xitong Zeng,Zhaoge Bi,Yitian Yang,Huaming Chen,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudBench，一个针对金融欺诈与信用风险检测中对抗鲁棒性评估的协议敏感型基准。该基准在三种匹配的评估协议下（无约束攻击、事后可行性过滤、部署感知的约束集成攻击）评估同一数据集-模型-攻击-防御设置，揭示了评估协议对鲁棒性结论的显著影响。实验表明，不同协议下可行扰动样本数量差异巨大，且攻击可行性与攻击者能力是两个独立维度，协议选择甚至可改变模型家族的性能排名。因此，应联合报告预测退化与攻击可行性，并将领域约束融入攻击生成过程而非作为事后处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融表格数据中的对抗鲁棒性评估面临领域特定约束、严重类别不平衡和非对称攻击者能力等挑战，现有方法常将约束视为事后验证，导致评估结果不可靠。因此需要一种能反映评估协议影响的系统性基准。

Method: 提出 FraudBench 基准，涵盖四个公开金融数据集，评估神经网络、树模型及集成模型，采用三种攻击设置：无约束攻击、事后可行性过滤、部署感知的约束集成攻击，系统比较不同协议下的鲁棒性表现。

Result: 在 Lending Club 数据集上，后处理过滤仅保留平均 3.7 个可行扰动样本，而攻击中投影结合攻击者可变性掩码则产生 2,832.3 个；在 IEEE-CIS 数据集上，可行性与攻击者能力可分离；黑盒评估显示协议选择会改变模型家族排序。

Conclusion: 金融欺诈场景下的鲁棒性评估必须将领域约束整合进攻击生成过程，而非作为事后检查；评估应同时报告预测性能下降与攻击可行性，并强调评估协议的重要性。

Abstract: Machine learning models are widely used in financial fraud and credit-risk detection, yet their adversarial robustness remains difficult to evaluate because financial tabular data involve domain-specific constraints, severe class imbalance, and asymmetric attacker capability. We argue that, in this setting, robustness is not only an attribute of the model, but also an attribute of the evaluation protocol. Different ways of enforcing constraints and capability can lead to substantially different robustness conclusions. This paper presents FraudBench, a protocol-sensitive benchmark for adversarial robustness evaluation in financial fraud and credit-risk detection. Rather than treating domain constraints as post-hoc validity checks, FraudBench evaluates the same dataset--model--attack--defence setting under three matched protocols: unconstrained attacks, post-hoc feasibility filtering, and deployment-aware constraint-integrated attacks. FraudBench covers four public financial datasets, and evaluates neural, tree-based, and ensemble models using three attack settings. Our results show that robustness conclusions are highly protocol-sensitive. On Lending Club Loan Data under the white-box setting, post-hoc filtering leaves only 3.7 feasible-flipped examples on average, whereas in-attack projection with attacker mutability masking produces 2,832.3 feasible-flipped examples under the same perturbation budget. The results on IEEE-CIS further show that feasibility and attacker capability are separate axes, while black-box evaluation shows that protocol choice can alter model-family rankings. These findings suggest that fraud robustness evaluation should report predictive degradation and attack feasibility jointly, and should incorporate domain constraints into attack generation rather than treating them as post-processing checks.

</details>


### [211] [SeisMamba: Low-Latency Single-Station Seismic Magnitude Estimation for Spatially Distributed Earthquake Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24561)
*Quenton Yeo,Zhaoge Bi,Linghan Huang,Luke Stephen Higgins,Flora Salim,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeisMamba 是一种基于 Mamba 架构的轻量级模型，用于从单站记录的三通道地震波形中实现低延迟、高精度的地震震级估计。它结合了分层卷积编码、稀疏选择性状态空间建模、多尺度特征融合和辅助时间预测头，能够在仅 0.55 毫秒处理 32 个波形批次的情况下，显著优于现有方法，且在跨区域测试中表现稳健，适用于传感器稀疏地区的低成本地震预警系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震早期预警系统依赖密集的区域地震网络和区域特异性校准，难以覆盖传感器稀疏的高风险地区。单站学习虽为低成本替代方案，但普遍存在精度与延迟之间的权衡，且在区域分布变化下性能下降。因此亟需一种高效、鲁棒且可跨区域部署的单站震级估计算法。

Method: SeisMamba 采用分层卷积编码提取局部特征，引入稀疏选择性状态空间模型（Mamba）进行长序列建模，结合多尺度特征融合机制，并设计辅助时间预测头以增强时序建模能力，从而实现对原始三通道地震波形的高效分析。

Result: 在 STEAD 基准测试中，SeisMamba 在 MSE、RMSE 和 $R^2$ 指标上均优于现有基线模型；在 NVIDIA T4 GPU 上每批 32 个波形仅需 0.55 毫秒，速度约为基于 Transformer 的方法的三倍。跨区域（智利-台湾）测试表明其在地理未见区域仍保持良好性能，具备强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模为构建空间分布广泛、成本低廉的地震早期预警系统提供了兼具高精度与低延迟的可行技术路径，SeisMamba 展现出在稀疏监测区部署的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Rapid earthquake magnitude estimation is central to earthquake early warning, yet many operational systems depend on dense regional seismic networks and region-specific calibration. This creates a spatial coverage barrier for high-risk areas with sparse sensing infrastructure. Single-station learning offers a lower-cost alternative, but existing models often face an accuracy--latency trade-off and may degrade under regional distribution shift. We present SeisMamba, a lightweight Mamba-based architecture for low-latency magnitude estimation from minimally processed three-component seismic waveforms recorded at a single station. SeisMamba combines hierarchical convolutional encoding, sparse selective state-space modelling, multi-scale feature fusion, and an auxiliary temporal prediction head to support efficient long-sequence waveform analysis. On the STEAD benchmark, SeisMamba achieves the best MSE, RMSE, and $R^2$ among tested baselines while requiring only 0.55 ms for a batch of 32 waveforms on an NVIDIA T4 GPU, making it about three times faster than transformer-based baselines. We further conduct a Chile--Taiwan regional hold-out experiment as a diagnostic test of cross-region deployment, where SeisMamba retains useful performance on geographically unseen seismic regions. These results suggest that selective state-space waveform modelling provides a promising accuracy--latency backbone for spatially distributed, low-cost earthquake early warning.

</details>


### [212] [Across the Loss Landscape with Progressive Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24568)
*Paul Caillon,Christophe Cerisara,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究增量式增长与优化策略如何通过渐进约束松弛机制，使训练偏向更平坦的损失区域。通过逐步解锁嵌套的随机子空间并冻结正交补空间，作者在理论和实验上证明了该方法能增强宽基域的相对权重，抑制尖锐基域，从而解释了深度神经网络的良好泛化能力。尽管该方法能稳定产生更平坦的解，但平坦性降低并不总带来测试性能提升，揭示了平坦性与泛化之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络为何能在高度非凸且过参数化的损失景观中实现良好泛化，特别是探究增量式训练策略如何引导优化过程趋向更平坦的极小值区域。

Method: 采用增量式生长策略，从低维子模型开始，逐步解锁嵌套随机子空间，同时冻结正交方向于初始状态，并在每次扩展后重新优化，直至达到完整网络结构；结合局部正则性假设，利用椭球近似子水平集，推导出冻结方向上的有效曲率，分析基域可访问性。

Result: 理论证明了在非退化极小值附近，子水平集可被椭球良好近似，冻结约束导致的体积效应使宽基域相对权重增加，从而解释了对平坦区域的偏好；实验验证了该机制在人工模型和真实ResNet/CIFAR-100设置中的有效性，发现平坦性改善不必然带来泛化性能提升。

Conclusion: 增量生长策略通过冻结方向的体积效应自然偏向平坦基域，这为深度网络泛化提供了几何解释；然而，平坦性与泛化之间并非简单线性关系，需谨慎对待其关联性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks generalize well despite their highly nonconvex, overparameterized loss landscapes, a phenomenon often associated with the geometry of the minima found by stochastic optimization. We study how incremental grow-and-optimize strategies bias training toward flatter regions by viewing growth as progressive constraint relaxation. Starting from a low-dimensional submodel, we iteratively expand the trainable parameters by unlocking nested random subspaces while freezing the orthogonal complement at the network initialization, re-optimizing after each expansion until the full architecture is reached. Under standard local regularity conditions around non-degenerate minima, we prove that local sublevel sets are well approximated by ellipsoids and that basin accessibility under frozen constraints can be characterized by an explicit effective curvature in the frozen directions. This leads to an explanation of the bias: progressive growth increases the relative weight of wide basins and suppresses sharp ones through a volume effect induced by the frozen constraints. We empirically validate these predictions in controlled toy landscapes and in a realistic ResNet/CIFAR-100 setting and confirm that although progressive subspace growth reliably produces flatter solutions, curvature reductions do not universally translate into improved test performance, highlighting subtleties in the flatness-generalization connection. The code is available at https://github.com/p0lcAi/Across-the-Loss-Landscape.

</details>


### [213] [IAPO: Influence-Aware Policy Optimization for Credit Assignment in Multi-Turn Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24588)
*Bo Ren,Yirong Mao,Yi Yang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为影响感知策略优化（IAPO）的新方法，通过构建可训练的行动依赖图来捕捉多轮交互中信息与错误的流动，利用轨迹级优势重新分配信用，从而在服务代理任务中实现更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互任务中，任务信息随时间逐步揭示，仅靠最终奖励难以判断各动作的贡献，现有方法依赖对比数据或额外学习信号，存在局限性。

Method: 将每个回滚表示为带有类型的影响-依赖图，以用户和工具观察作为证据，通过支持使用和失败使用的结构生成路由权重，动态重分配轨迹级优势。

Result: 在{τ^2}-Bench、UserBench和AgentChangeBench三个基准上，Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B均优于多轮强化学习基线；BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn验证了其在多轮函数调用上的兼容性。

Conclusion: IAPO提供了一种基于稀疏结果反馈的系统性信用分配方法，显著提升了服务代理在复杂多轮交互中的训练效果，推动了对多轮交互中信用分配的理解。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly solve long-horizon tasks through multi-turn interactions with users and external tools. In these settings, relevant task information often unfolds over time rather than being fully specified at the initial prompt. Service agents make this challenge especially concrete: users may clarify or revise their goals, while tool responses provide information needed for subsequent decisions. Thus, a final reward alone cannot indicate which actions contributed to resolving the task. Recent methods rely on comparative evidence from other trajectories or resampled continuations, or on separately constructed step-level learning signals, to refine credit. However, a completed rollout already records how information and errors flow between agent actions. We introduce Influence-Aware Policy Optimization (IAPO), which represents each rollout as a typed influence-dependency graph over trainable agent actions, with user and tool observations serving as evidence. IAPO converts support-use and failed-use structure into routing weights that redistribute the same trajectory-level advantage. Experiments with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B demonstrate superior performance over multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) baselines across three service-agent benchmarks: {τ^2}-Bench, UserBench, and AgentChangeBench. BFCL-v4 Multi-Turn further shows that these gains do not compromise multi-turn function-calling performance. This work advances the understanding of credit assignment in multi-turn user interactions and provides a principled approach to training service agents from sparse outcome feedback.

</details>


### [214] [Delayed Optimizer-State Transport Shapes Short-Horizon Training Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24593)
*Jinhui Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了自适应优化器中梯度历史记忆（如AdamW）对短期训练决策的影响，通过分析8步轨迹和损失调度，在12个未使用的历史中发现完整梯度传输相比即时导数能显著降低令牌不相交的损失（10/12情况下有效，平均收益4.71×10⁻⁴，单侧符号检验p=0.0193）。实验表明优化器状态与近未来数据顺序的交互导致策略选择差异，且移除动量状态传输会破坏预测准确性。结果强调优化器记忆与未来数据顺序是可操作的训练状态成分，为是否采用有限时域干预而非单步干预提供了机制依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自适应优化器中延迟的梯度历史（即动量状态）是否足以影响短期训练决策，从而判断何时需要采用多步而非单步干预策略。

Method: 在已确定的未来小批量序列上，对AdamW的8步轨迹进行端到端反向传播，结合模型-优化器状态，筛选匹配暴露的数学-代码损失调度，并独立评估；通过交叉检查点-未来路径测试验证优化器状态与未来数据的相互作用，辅以独立的Ising-CNN实验验证动量状态传输的重要性。

Result: 在12个0.3M Transformer历史中，完整梯度传输比优化器感知的即时导数在10/12次中表现更优，平均提升4.71×10⁻⁴；60/96窗口中控制器选择不同调度；移除动量状态传输后预测准确率下降；大候选库中全传输得分更集中于精确回放胜者。

Conclusion: 优化器记忆与近未来数据顺序是训练状态中的可操作组件，其交互作用决定了应采用有限时域干预而非单步干预的机制基础。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers retain gradient history in moment variables, allowing a local change in loss weighting to alter later updates. We examine whether this delayed transport is large enough to change prospective short-horizon decisions. On committed future-minibatch sequences, we differentiate eight-step AdamW trajectories through the complete model--optimizer state and select exposure-matched Math--Code loss schedules before independent evaluation. Across 12 unused 0.3M Transformer histories, full transport lowers token-disjoint loss relative to an optimizer-aware immediate derivative in 10/12 histories (mean benefit $4.71\times10^{-4}$; exact one-sided sign test, $p=0.0193$). The two controllers act equally often but select different schedules in 60/96 windows. Crossed checkpoint--future-path tests attribute this reordering to the interaction between optimizer state and near-future data, while an independent Ising--CNN experiment shows that deleting moment-state transport destroys accurate response prediction. Full-transport scores also concentrate exact-rollout winners in larger candidate libraries, focusing finite-amplitude evaluation on a shortlist. On these committed short paths, optimizer memory and near-future data order are therefore actionable components of the training state, providing a mechanism-based criterion for when finite-horizon rather than one-step intervention is required.

</details>


### [215] [Taming foundation model with invariance-oriented pre-training for broad-spectrum EEG analysis across signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24597)
*Yulong Dou,Han Wu,Guo Chen,Fangmao Ju,Zhiming Cui,Dinggang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INCEPT is an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabeled clinical EEG data. Unlike reconstruction-focused models, INCEPT emphasizes representation-level stability across correlated EEG signals, separating stable neural structure from nuisance variability while preserving subject-, state-, and condition-specific information. Evaluated across ten datasets spanning signal-level, brain-state, and brain-health tasks, INCEPT outperforms other EEG foundation models and specialist encoders on most metrics, demonstrating the power of invariance learning for transferable EEG representations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing EEG models are typically tied to a one-dataset-one-model paradigm, limiting reusability. While recent EEG foundation models focus on signal reconstruction, they assume that predictable content from local context equals transferable neural information—this assumption may not hold. The authors aim to develop a more robust, transferable representation by prioritizing invariance across correlated EEG observations.

Method: INCEPT uses an invariance-oriented pre-training strategy on large-scale unlabeled clinical EEG data (11,000+ hours). It learns stable representations by identifying consistent patterns across correlated EEG signals, effectively disentangling stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive features from noise and variability inherent in scalp recordings.

Result: INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 out of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 out of 30 fine-tuning metrics. It also surpasses specialized encoders across diverse downstream tasks. Ablation studies confirm that invariance learning enhances transferability and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations better than reconstruction-only approaches.

Conclusion: Invariance learning provides a powerful principle for building reusable EEG foundation models. By focusing on stable, transferable neural representations rather than signal reconstruction alone, INCEPT enables superior performance across a wide range of EEG analysis tasks.

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used window into human brain function, but most EEG models remain tied to a one-dataset-one-model supervised paradigm. Recent EEG foundation models offer a route toward reusable representations, but most remain reconstruction-centered, assuming that EEG content predictable from local context is necessarily transferable neural information. Here we present INCEPT, an invariance-oriented EEG foundation model trained on over 11,000 hours of unlabelled clinical EEG. Rather than prioritizing signal recovery alone, INCEPT learns representation-level stability across correlated EEG observations, separating stable neural structure and essential subject-sensitive information from the nuisance variability that dominates scalp recordings while preserving subject-, state- and condition-discriminative information. We evaluate INCEPT on a broad-spectrum benchmark of ten datasets spanning three levels of post-acquisition EEG analysis: signal-level assessment, brain-state decoding, and brain-health evaluation. INCEPT ranks first among recent EEG foundation models on 26 of 30 linear-probing metrics and 24 of 30 fine-tuning metrics, and also surpasses strong task-specific specialist encoders across diverse downstream settings. Objective ablations and representation analyses further show that invariance-oriented pre-training improves transfer and organizes subject-sensitive neural representations beyond reconstruction alone. These results establish invariance learning as a promising principle for building reusable EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [216] [Conditional GraphGANFed: Optimizing Graph-Structured Molecule Generation in Federated Generative Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24610)
*Daniel Manu,Abee Alazzwi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的条件图生成对抗网络联邦学习框架cGraphGANFed，通过引入评鉴网络来评估生成分子在用户自定义指标上的表现，并将评鉴结果与判别器反馈共同融入生成器损失函数，从而生成在化学性质相似的同时优化特定目标指标的新分子。在多种场景下实验表明，cGraphGANFed在有效性、LogP和QED等指标上显著优于GraphGANFed，且对非独立同分布数据和模式崩溃具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphGANFed无法根据用户定义的指标生成优化分子，限制了其在新药发现中的应用，因此需要一种能够同时保持化学性质相似性并优化特定指标的生成方法。

Method: 在GraphGANFed基础上引入评鉴网络，结合判别器输出与评鉴网络对生成分子的评分，联合构建生成器的损失函数，实现对用户定义指标的条件控制生成。

Result: 在多场景测试中，cGraphGANFed在有效性、LogP和QED等关键指标上均优于GraphGANFed，尤其在仅优化QED时提升超过10%；同时具备更强的抗非IID数据干扰和防止模式崩溃的能力。

Conclusion: cGraphGANFed有效解决了传统GraphGANFed无法按需优化分子属性的问题，为隐私保护下的高效分子生成提供了新方案，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have garnered considerable attention in molecular discovery for their ability to generate novel and high-quality molecules. To efficiently train a GAN model while preserving data privacy, GraphGANFed has been proposed to incorporate federated learning and graph convolutional networks into GAN. Yet, GraphGANFed cannot produce synthetic molecules that only optimize a user-defined metric(s) to facilitate the new drug discovery process. To address this issue, we introduce a novel extension to GraphGANFed, namely conditional GraphGANFed (cGraphGANFed), by incorporating the critic network to assess generated molecules using user-defined metric(s). The evaluation results from both the critic network and discriminator are integrated into the loss function of the generator, guiding it to generate novel molecules that maintain similar chemical properties to real ones while optimizing user-defined metrics. Extensive simulations are conducted in two scenarios. First, cGraphGANFed endeavors to optimize all seven commonly used metrics, and the results show that cGraphGANFed significantly outperforms GraphGANFed in Validity and LogP, with a slight advantage in QED, across different settings. Second, cGraphGANFed focuses solely on optimizing QED, and the results show that the synthetic molecules produced by cGraphGANFed can achieve more than 10% improvement in QED than GraphGANFed. Also, the results demonstrate cGraphGANFed has enhanced resilience against mode collapses and performance reduction caused by non-IID data.

</details>


### [217] [Bandit Submodular Maximization under Matroid Constraints: Learning Compressed Exchange Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24627)
*Zongqi Wan,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在拟阵约束下的对抗性随机带子问题中单调次模函数的最大化。针对秩为k的拟阵，提出了一种随机的、基于预言机的多项式时间算法，每轮仅需一次可行值查询，其期望(1-1/e)-遗憾为~O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})，是首个在一般拟阵约束下实现亚线性遗憾的对抗性随机带子次模最大化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在对抗性随机带子设置下对一般拟阵约束的次模最大化无法实现亚线性遗憾，缺乏高效且具有理论保证的算法。本文旨在解决这一关键挑战。

Method: 将问题视为学习泊松基行走的交换策略；引入平衡分数交换机制，将指数级策略混合压缩为单一分数基，保留泊松分析所需交换信息，从而设计出多项式时间算法。

Result: 提出的新算法实现了~O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})的期望(1-1/e)-遗憾，首次在一般拟阵约束下达到亚线性遗憾，且运行时间与空间复杂度均为多项式。

Conclusion: 通过引入平衡分数交换机制，成功将复杂的策略学习问题转化为可计算的分数基表示，不仅理论上实现了亚线性遗憾，还保持了高效的计算性能，为对抗性随机带子次模优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandit maximization of monotone submodular functions under a matroid constraint. For a rank-$k$ matroid on $n$ elements, we give a randomized oracle-polynomial algorithm that makes one feasible value query per round and has expected $(1-1/e)$-regret $\widetilde O(n^{1/3}k^{2/3}T^{2/3})$. This is the first sublinear-regret algorithm for adversarial bandit submodular maximization under general matroid constraints.
  Technically, we view the problem as learning an exchange policy for the Poisson base walk. This connects the problem to contextual bandits and gives an information-theoretic sublinear-regret guarantee, but directly learning the exponentially many policies requires exponential time and space. We therefore introduce \emph{balanced fractional exchanges}, which compress the policy mixture into a single fractional base while retaining the exchange information needed by the Poisson analysis. This leads to an polynomial time algorithm with the same regret guarantee.

</details>


### [218] [Data Leakage Inflates Generalizability of Power Outage Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24665)
*Yamil Essus,Ranga Raju Vatsavai,Benjamin Rachunok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力中断预测模型在气候驱动的基础设施风险评估中的泛化能力，指出当前方法学选择（如随机划分、空间/时间留出策略）会影响模型在新条件下的表现。通过美国东海岸2018-2023年的公开数据与气象再分析、土地覆盖及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）嵌入特征进行对比，发现随机分割下模型表现良好，但因时空自相关导致结果被夸大；在真实部署情境（如留一州/事件）下，性能显著下降，常无法超越简单基线。尽管引入GeoAI嵌入对空间泛化略有提升，但对事件级迁移帮助有限。结论认为，当前数据与评估方式限制了模型的实用价值，需改进数据覆盖、采用更真实评估协议，并关注结构性数据瓶颈而非仅追求模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力中断预测模型虽广泛用于气候风险评估，但其评估方法常掩盖模型在新型条件下的泛化能力，尤其在空间、时间及事件层面的适应性不足，亟需系统检验其真实部署潜力。

Method: 使用美国东海岸2018–2023年公开数据，结合天气再分析、土地覆盖数据及GeoAI基础模型（Prithvi WxC）生成的嵌入特征，比较不同训练-测试划分策略（包括无筛选随机分割、留一州出、留一事件出）下的模型表现，评估其在不同场景下的泛化能力。

Result: 随机分割下模型表现优异，但受空间与时间自相关影响，结果被高估；在留一州和留一事件等更具现实意义的测试设计中，模型性能大幅下降，多数情况下无法超越简单基线；引入GeoAI嵌入仅带来有限且不一致的改进，主要体现在空间泛化，未能解决事件层面的迁移问题。

Conclusion: 当前数据可用性和评估实践下，公开训练的停电预测模型具有有限且不确定的操作价值。未来进展需依赖更好的数据覆盖、更真实的评估协议，并从追求细微建模优化转向解决结构性数据约束问题。

Abstract: Power outage prediction models are increasingly used in assessments of climate-driven infrastructure risk, yet current evaluation practices obscure whether these models generalize to the novel conditions such applications require. We identify three common methodological choices in power outage prediction models that influence their ability to generalize across spatial, temporal, and event-based settings. We compare the predictive performance impacts of different methodological decisions using publicly available data for the U.S. East Coast from 2018 to 2023 and feature sets derived from weather reanalysis and land-cover data, and embeddings from a GeoAI foundation model (Prithvi WxC). Specifically, we assess model performance under multiple test selection strategies, including unfiltered random splits, leave-one-state-out, and leave-one-event-out designs, which increasingly approximate real-world deployment conditions. While random train-test splits yield strong performance, we show that these results are inflated by spatial and temporal autocorrelation. Under spatial and temporal holdout experiments, predictive accuracy degrades substantially, with models often failing to outperform a simple null baseline. Incorporating GeoAI foundation model embeddings yields limited and inconsistent improvements, primarily for spatial generalization, and does not resolve poor event-level transferability. These findings suggest that, given current data availability and evaluation practices, publicly trained outage prediction models offer limited and uncertain operational value. Progress will likely require improved data coverage, more realistic evaluation protocols, and a shift in focus from marginal modeling advances toward addressing structural data constraints.

</details>


### [219] [A Multimodal Foundation Model for Longitudinal Patient Representation and Scalable Insight Generation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24688)
*Eugene Vorontsov,Yi Kan Wang,Alican Bozkurt,Adam Casson,Ludmila Tydlitatova,Michal Zelechowski,Ezra E. W. Cohen,Jyoti D. Patel,Max Banaszak,Caitlin McWilliams,Shane Colley,Kate Sasser,Ryan Fukushima,Eric Lefkofsky,Razik Yousfi,Siqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: oFM 是一个基于167万癌症患者真实世界队列的肿瘤学基础模型，整合了临床轨迹与DNA、RNA及组织病理图像数据，通过编码每日临床和分子事件，生成患者状态嵌入。在预后评估中，oFM显著提升治疗反应、无进展生存和总生存的AUC（0.774 vs. 0.563），在11个对比治疗队列中，其治疗效益AUTOC比基线高三倍，并在9个队列中改善了效益排序。此外，该模型支持机制发现框架，通过时间图谱揭示预测结果与临床/生物学机制的关联，适用于临床和药物开发应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需要能够捕捉癌症演化与治疗过程的纵向患者状态模型，整合多模态数据以实现更准确的预后与治疗响应预测。

Method: 构建oFM基础模型，利用167万患者数据训练，采用患者级划分进行训练、验证与测试；模型编码每日临床与分子事件，结合病理图像，生成患者状态嵌入；通过冻结的嵌入向量与专家标注的基线特征进行比较，并引入机制发现框架分析预测结果的生物学解释性。

Result: oFM在治疗反应、无进展生存和总生存预测中显著优于基线（AUC 0.774 vs. 0.563）；在11个治疗队列中，治疗效益的AUTOC提升三倍，9个队列中排名优化；在两组治疗臂内均提供更强的预后区分能力；机制发现框架可将预测结果与临床/生物机制关联，支持临床与药物研发应用。

Conclusion: oFM是一种强大的纵向多模态基础模型，能有效整合临床、分子与病理数据，提升癌症预后与治疗响应预测性能，并具备可解释性机制发现能力，具有广泛应用于精准肿瘤学和药物开发的潜力。

Abstract: Precision oncology necessitates a longitudinal model of patient state that captures cancer evolution and treatment over time, integrating multimodal observations. We introduce the oFM, a foundation model developed on a real-world oncology cohort of 1.67 million cancer patients that integrates clinical trajectories with DNA, RNA, and H&E pathology. Patient-level partitions were reserved for training, validation, and testing, with over one million patients used for training. The oFM encodes daily clinical and molecular episodes and, along with pathology images, integrates them over time to produce a patient state embedding. We evaluate frozen oFM embeddings against expert-curated clinical and molecular baseline features. In prognostic benchmarks, the oFM improved AUC for treatment response, progression-free survival, and overall survival (0.774 vs. 0.563 for overall survival). Across 11 comparative-treatment cohorts, the oFM embeddings achieved a three-fold higher pooled and scale-normalized treatment-benefit AUTOC than baseline features with improved benefit ranking in 9 of 11 cohorts, and provided stronger prognostic discrimination within both treatment arms. We also evaluated a mechanism discovery framework that interprets downstream models built on oFM embeddings by linking their predicted outcomes to clinically and biologically grounded mechanisms through an evidence-grounded temporal graph, enabling evaluation in clinical and drug-development applications.

</details>


### [220] [On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696)
*Wenze Lin,Jiale Zhao,Xitai Jiang,Songde Rao,Yining Li,Shenzhi Wang,Bingxiang He,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPDVR 是一种无需额外超参数即可无缝结合 on-policy distillation (OPD) 与可验证奖励强化学习 (RLVR) 的新方法。通过重构采样标记的隐式奖励并引入 ReLU 门控机制，确保正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励，从而对齐任务成功目标并保留教师分布指导。该方法还将 OPD 转化为正规的 RLVR 框架，兼容如 GRPO 等策略梯度算法。在六个推理基准上的实验表明，OPDVR 显著优于标准 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法结合 OPD 与 RLVR 时依赖加权组合或启发式切换，引入额外超参数和权衡；而 OPD 缺乏轨迹正确性考量，RLVR 则面临任务级反馈稀疏问题。因此需要一种无需调参、能同时利用密集监督与任务正确性的融合方法。

Method: 提出 OPDVR：首先基于轨迹正确性重构 OPD 的隐式奖励；然后引入 ReLU 门控机制，使正确轨迹获得非负奖励、错误轨迹获得非正奖励；该设计使 OPD 兼具任务导向性与教师分布保持能力，并可自然接入任意策略梯度算法（如 GRPO）。

Result: 在六个推理基准测试中，OPDVR 均显著优于标准 OPD，在性能上实现了稳定提升，且无需任何额外超参数调节。

Conclusion: OPDVR 成功实现 OPD 与 RLVR 的无损融合，兼具密集监督与任务正确性引导，是一种简单高效、无需调参的后训练优化方案，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two widely adopted paradigms for post-training large language models. However, RLVR suffers from sparse task-level feedback, while OPD provides dense token-level guidance but ignores trajectory correctness, limiting its performance to that of the teacher. Combining them is a promising direction: OPD supplies dense supervisory signals, while RLVR provides task-level correctness. Nevertheless, existing integrations often rely on weighted combination or heuristic switching, introducing extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. We propose On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward (OPDVR), a simple yet effective method that seamlessly combines OPD and RLVR without adding any hyperparameters. We first reformulate the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD based on trajectory correctness, then apply a ReLU gating mechanism to ensure that correct trajectories receive non-negative rewards and incorrect ones receive non-positive rewards---thereby aligning the distillation signal with task success while preserving the teacher's distributional guidance. Furthermore, our modification transforms sampled-token OPD into a proper RLVR method, making it readily combinable with any policy gradient algorithm, such as GRPO. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that OPDVR consistently outperforms standard OPD. Our code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR.

</details>


### [221] [Single State Update Predictive Coding training for Time Series Forecasting and Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24697)
*Matteo Cardoni,Sam Leroux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型训练技术，将生成式预测编码网络（Generative PCN）与支持编码预测编码网络（Encoding PCN）并行训练，以避免传统预测编码网络中串行反向误差传播的瓶颈，从而实现更稳定、连续的在线学习，应用于时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决预测编码网络中因串行反向误差传播导致的训练瓶颈问题，提升训练效率与在线学习稳定性。

Method: 引入生成式预测编码网络与支持编码预测编码网络，二者并行训练以匹配神经激活，避免依赖串行误差传播。

Result: 在时间序列异常检测任务中，该方法实现了更稳定、连续的在线学习性能。

Conclusion: 通过并行化训练机制，该方法有效克服了传统预测编码网络的串行计算瓶颈，显著提升了模型在在线学习场景下的表现。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is a neural learning paradigm that enables parallelizable neural network layer updates. However, the main bottleneck of PC Networks (PCN) is the sequential backwards error propagation. To tackle this, we introduce a training technique that pairs a Generative PCN with a support Encoding PCN. The two PCNs are trained in parallel to match their neural activations, without sequential propagation. We apply this to time series anomaly detection and show that our approach results in more stable, continuous, online learning.

</details>


### [222] [Parameter-Level Attribution of Symmetry in Trained Networks Though Parameter-Wise Functional Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24700)
*Alan Muriithi,Vedanta Thapar,Torben Berndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究网络参数化中对称性如何在函数空间中实现，提出通过参数空间动作实现函数空间群作用的提升问题。关键条件是函数对称轨道的切空间需位于参数映射导数的像空间内，即功能敏感度的列空间中。若满足该条件，可实现点态一阶提升；否则可通过最小二乘法找到两个局部方向：一个沿对称轨道，一个朝等变子空间下降，残差反映参数化无法达到的部分。实验表明，在旋转不变分类器和哈密顿神经网络上，这些方向能局部实现预期的函数空间运动，但仅限于训练初期，固定方向会随训练偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当网络学习具有已知对称性的函数时，该对称性是否能在参数空间中被有效表示和移动，即是否存在参数空间中的动作能对应函数空间中的对称操作。这有助于理解神经网络对称性建模的能力与局限。

Method: 将对称性实现问题形式化为函数映射 $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$ 的提升问题，分析参数空间动作存在的必要条件：函数对称轨道的切空间必须包含在 $\mathrm{d}Φ_θ$ 的像中。通过功能敏感度矩阵的列空间判断条件是否满足，并使用最小二乘方法松弛条件，求解两个局部参数方向。

Result: 在旋转不变分类器和旋转对称势能的哈密顿神经网络实验中，所推导的两个参数方向能局部实现函数空间的对称运动；重新计算的方向能持续跟踪对称轨道并减少等变缺陷，而固定方向则在训练后逐渐偏离。结果验证了理论条件的局部有效性。

Conclusion: 只有当函数对称轨道的切空间位于参数映射导数的像中时，才能实现平滑的参数空间对称动作。这一条件在理论上是一阶提升的充要条件，实验表明其在局部范围内有效，且对不对称架构仍具解释力。

Abstract: When a network has learned a function with a known symmetry, can that symmetry be moved through the parametrisation---is there a motion in parameter space realising the group action in function space? We formulate this as a lifting problem for the realisation map $Φ:θ\mapsto f_θ$, and show that a smooth parameter-space action exists only if the tangent space to the function's symmetry orbit lies within the image of $\mathrm dΦ_θ$, whose columns are the \emph{functional sensitivities} of individual parameters. This condition is also sufficient for pointwise first-order lifting. Relaxing it in least squares yields two local parameter directions: one following the symmetry orbit, one descending towards the equivariant subspace, with residuals measuring what the parametrisation cannot reach. On a rotationally invariant classifier we find these directions induce their predicted function-space motion, but only locally: recomputed directions track the orbit and reduce the equivariance defect, while directions held fixed depart from both after training. The same holds for Hamiltonian neural networks trained on a rotationally symmetric potential, even though the architecture does not explicitly enforce the symmetry.

</details>


### [223] [Constrained Hyperparameter Optimization for Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24712)
*Bruno Veloso,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线优化算法中边界约束的处理策略，提出四种新方法以改进传统“边界”策略。实验表明，新策略在标准数据集上表现更优，能有效提升超参数优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于批处理学习，而数据流场景下的超参数优化面临挑战，尤其需要在在线学习中实现自适应调整。由于超参数受边界限制，传统优化操作易产生无效解，因此需有效处理边界约束。

Method: 提出四种边界约束处理策略，用于解决在线优化中因超参数越界导致的无效解问题。通过实证分析比较不同策略的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提策略优于传统的“边界”策略，在多个基准数据集上实现了更高的优化性能。

Conclusion: 在在线优化中，有效的边界约束处理策略对提升超参数优化效果至关重要；本文提出的策略显著优于现有方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Optimization of hyperparameters is a critical factor to obtain optimal model performance. While existing research has predominantly concentrated on batch-learning scenarios, addressing the complexities inherent in data streams presents a challenge. The deployment of sophisticated methodologies to manage data streams becomes highly important. Consequently, the capacity for self-adjusting hyperparameters during on-line learning phases emerges as a goal. Many hyperparameters exhibit constraints and are confined within bounded search spaces, rendering specific solutions unacceptable upon applying optimization operators. To solve this issue, employing boundary constraint- handling techniques becomes imperative to rectify invalid solutions. This paper presents strategies for effectively managing boundary constraints within constrained numerical optimization problems. Recent methodologies, including heuristic and evolutionary-based optimization, employ a "boundary" strategy, wherein values that surpass boundary thresholds for a given hyperparameter are realigned to the respective limits. Our study introduces four strategies to navigate boundary constraints in online optimization algorithms. Through empirical investigations conducted on established datasets, we demonstrate that adopting boundary strategies outperforms the "boundary" strategy.

</details>


### [224] [Enhancing Bayesian Optimization and Active Learning Through Kernel Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24721)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Konstantinos D. Polyzos,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一框架KENDO（Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator），通过集成高斯过程（EGP）与基于分歧感知的采集策略，替代传统的超参数采样，实现高效且准确的贝叶斯优化（BO）和贝叶斯主动学习（AL）。KENDO-BO和KENDO-AL均源于同一自校正机制，具有任务特定的采集目标，并可扩展至多目标优化。实验表明，KENDO-BO在性能上优于或相当主流方法，计算开销降低达5倍；KENDO-AL在预测校准性上优于基于MCMC的方法，速度提升最高达27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数选择是贝叶斯优化和贝叶斯主动学习中的关键挑战，模型误设会导致性能下降，而更精确的全贝叶斯方法通常依赖计算昂贵的MCMC采样，亟需高效且鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 提出KENDO框架，采用核集成（kernel ensemble）和自适应贝叶斯加权替代超参数采样，并结合分歧感知的采集策略；构建KENDO-BO和KENDO-AL，利用随机标量化处理多目标优化，保持单优化器条件结构。

Result: KENDO-BO在多个基准测试中达到与先进方法相当或更优的优化性能，同时计算成本降低最多5倍；KENDO-AL在预测校准方面优于基于MCMC的基线，速度提升最高达27倍。

Conclusion: KENDO提供了一个高效、统一且可扩展的框架，有效缓解了超参数敏感性和计算成本问题，在单目标、多目标优化和主动学习任务中均表现出色。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection remains a key challenge in Bayesian optimization (BO) and Bayesian active learning (AL), as model misspecification can lead to suboptimal performance, while more accurate fully Bayesian treatments typically rely on computationally expensive MCMC sampling. This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [225] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限的临床环境中，通过仅更新9%参数的参数高效自监督适应方法是否足以使脑电图（EEG）基础模型（EEG-FM）在多种临床数据集上实现有效的表示对齐。实验评估了两种具有不同预训练目标的先进模型（BIOT 和 CBraMod），并在三个临床EEG数据集上进行异常检测（TUAB）、事件分类（TUEV）和癫痫发作检测（CHB-MIT）任务，涵盖分布内与分布外场景。结果表明，自监督适应相比线性探测有显著提升，最高可达到20倍的AUCPR增益；在固定计算预算下，仅需20%-50%的未标注数据即可达到峰值性能。关键发现是：当总窗口数固定时，性能不随患者数量变化，说明性能主要依赖于整体时间窗口的多样性而非患者数量。该研究证明，参数高效适应可在极低计算开销和数据收集负担下有效部署EEG基础模型。代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自监督学习的EEG基础模型虽具备可迁移表征潜力，但在跨多样临床数据集上的泛化能力仍有限；全微调因计算成本过高，在资源受限的临床环境中不可行。因此亟需一种高效、轻量化的适配方法以实现实际应用。

Method: 采用参数高效自监督适应策略，仅更新模型中9%的参数，结合对比学习（BIOT）与掩码重建（CBraMod）两类预训练模型，在多个临床EEG数据集上进行异常检测、事件分类与癫痫发作检测任务，并在分布内与分布外条件下评估性能。同时分析不同未标注数据量与患者数量对性能的影响。

Result: 自监督适应在所有任务中均显著优于线性探测，最大提升达20倍AUCPR；在固定计算预算下，仅需20%-50%的未标注数据即可达到最佳性能；当总时间窗口数固定时，性能不受患者数量影响，表明性能取决于时间窗口的多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效自监督适应能够以极小的计算开销和数据需求，有效实现EEG基础模型在临床场景中的部署，为实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [226] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中交替后悔的极小极大最优问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，提出了一个具有$O(\log d)$交替后悔的算法，且该后悔值在任意时间跨度$T$下保持常数，优于之前$O(\log^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$的结果。由此实现了在双人零和博弈中以$O(\log d / T)$收敛至纳什均衡，在双人一般和博弈中以$O(\log d / T)$收敛至联合相关均衡，这是首个实现$O(1/T)$收敛到联合相关均衡且无额外$\log T$因子的非耦合学习动态。对于一般OCO，给出了$O(d\log(1+T/d))$的交替后悔算法，并证明了匹配的下界，表明$\Omega(\log T)$因子不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交替学习动态中的后悔最小化问题，特别是在博弈论背景下的收敛速度改进，目标是设计更高效、更快速收敛的学习算法。

Method: 提出新的算法框架，结合对偶方法与自适应步长策略，针对OLO和OCO分别设计并分析其交替后悔上界，并通过构造对抗性损失序列证明下界。

Result: 在概率单纯形上，实现了$O(\log d)$的恒定交替后悔，显著优于先前结果；在一般凸集上，得到$O(d\log(1+T/d))$的交替后悔上界，并证明其紧性；首次获得$O(1/T)$收敛到联合相关均衡的非耦合动态，摆脱了$\log T$因子。

Conclusion: 本工作完成了交替后悔的极小极大最优性刻画，为在线学习与博弈中的快速收敛提供了理论基础，推动了非耦合学习动态的发展。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [227] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为$(\text{DNN})^2$的新方法，通过创新的特征值最大化程序在非唯一对偶乘子空间中搜索有效证书，以实现更紧致且可验证的神经网络安全性保证。该方法克服了传统双重非负（DNN）程序在规模化验证中的瓶颈，显著提升了与标准半定规划（SDP）相比的边界紧致性，并在许多情况下达到精确解，同时支持全局最优性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性规划和半定规划松弛在ReLU神经网络验证中因松弛间隙过大导致安全保证过于保守。虽然完全正定规划（CPP）能消除该间隙，但其求解是NP难的。而其最便宜的可处理松弛——双重非负规划（DNN）虽保留关键约束并具有更强紧致性，但其规模超出了内点法的实际应用范围。尽管Burer-Monteiro分解已用于提升基于SDP的验证可扩展性，但尚未适用于更严格的DNN形式。主要障碍在于DNN中的额外非负性约束导致对偶乘子不唯一，使标准认证方法失效。

Method: 提出一种新颖的特征值最大化过程，旨在探索由于非唯一性存在的对偶乘子空间，以寻找有效的优化证书，从而实现全局最优性保障。该方法结合了对偶结构分析与特征值优化策略，使得即使在乘子不唯一的情况下仍能构造出合法的验证证书。

Result: 实验表明，$(\text{DNN})^2$方法生成的界始终比标准SDP方法更紧，经常逼近甚至达到精确解；其认证流程可在存在有效证书时确认全局最优性。该方法为安全关键自主系统中部署神经网络控制器与感知模块提供了紧致、可验证且计算可扩展的验证保障。

Conclusion: $(\text{DNN})^2$成功将严格更优的DNN松弛转化为可实际应用的验证框架，解决了乘子非唯一性带来的认证难题，实现了紧致性、可验证性和可扩展性的统一，是迈向安全可信深度学习系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构（factor-space structure），用于区分具有不同几何特性的拓扑等价空间。传统方法使用欧几里得乘积坐标表示生成因子，但忽略了因子空间可能存在的缠绕、坍缩或位置依赖几何。本文揭示统计独立的因子未必在几何上可分离，例如色调与尺度的变化速率不同导致各向异性，无法通过固定重缩放消除。为此，提出因子空间拓扑图（FactoMap），学习由因子空间格点索引的可解释原型，通过拓扑学习将格点的周期性、坍缩和非均匀范围传递到表示中。实验表明，匹配该结构能保持因子连续性，并有效解耦潜在因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦方法多采用欧几里得乘积坐标表示生成因子，但忽略了因子空间可能具有的复杂几何结构（如缠绕、坍缩、位置依赖性）。这导致即使因子在统计上独立，也可能因几何不分离而难以实现真正的解耦。因此需要一种能够捕捉因子空间内在几何结构的方法。

Method: 引入因子空间结构，包含因子域、生成器诱导的识别关系以及位置依赖的尺度；提出因子空间拓扑图（FactoMap），通过学习基于因子空间格点的可解释原型，将格点的周期性、坍缩和非均匀扩展特性映射到表示中，实现对因子几何的建模。

Result: 实验表明，匹配因子空间结构的表示能有效保持因子的连续性，并显著提升对潜在生成因子的解耦能力，尤其在存在各向异性变化时表现更优。

Conclusion: 统计独立的因子不一定在几何上可分离，必须考虑因子空间的结构特性。通过显式建模因子空间结构（如周期性、坍缩、非均匀尺度），可实现更准确的解耦表示。FactoMap 有效利用了这些几何信息，为生成模型中的因子解耦提供了新范式。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [229] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LION提出一种基于克莱夫福德代数和解耦图神经范式的新型神经架构，用于多模态图学习中的对齐-融合任务。通过构建模态感知的几何流形实现高阶图传播以促进模态交互与对齐，并引入自适应全息聚合模块，结合拓扑感知的克莱夫福德分量，提升多模态融合能力。在9个文本-图像多模态学术图数据集上，LION在3项图任务和3项模态任务中均显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态图学习方法存在两大局限：一是模态对齐过程中忽视图上下文信息，导致模态间交互不足；二是模态融合缺乏对齐后拓扑先验的自适应利用，泛化能力差、性能下降。为此，亟需一种能有效实现上下文感知对齐与自适应融合的新范式。

Method: 提出LION框架，采用克莱夫福德代数构建模态感知的几何流形，实现高阶图传播以增强模态交互；采用解耦的传播-聚合范式，先进行模态对齐，再通过自适应全息聚合模块，融合组件能量与传播尺度信息，引入可学习参数以增强融合效果。

Result: 在9个文本-图像多模态学术图（MAG）数据集上的实验表明，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务中均显著超越现有最先进基线方法，验证了其优越性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: LION通过结合克莱夫福德代数与解耦图神经网络范式，有效解决了多模态图学习中模态对齐忽略上下文、融合缺乏适应性的问题，为多模态图表示学习提供了高效且通用的新范式。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [230] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MDTE提出一种少数类感知的扩散框架，通过条件扩散去噪重建稳定且具有区分性的时序边事件表示。该方法结合局部离群因子（LOF）传播过滤与聚类感知的低频传播，实现选择性传播以保留重要邻域依赖并减少多数类信息的同化；同时引入多视角判别融合机制，利用特征重构与拓扑预测捕捉类别间分布差异，提取互补判别信号指导去噪。在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，MDTE在少数类指标上显著优于现有基线，少数类召回率最高提升23.53个百分点，F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统节点和邻域信息在时序图中对少数类缺乏足够判别性，且多数类主导的时序传播会逐渐同化少数类表示，导致少数类分类性能下降。因此需要一种能够有效缓解信息同化、增强少数类表征能力的新方法。

Method: MDTE包含两个核心模块：1）分布感知的选择性传播（Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation），结合LOF进行传播过滤与聚类感知的低频传播，以保留关键邻居关系并抑制有害传播；2）多视角判别融合（Multi-View Discriminative Fusion），通过特征重构与拓扑预测联合学习，挖掘类别间的分布差异并提取互补判别信号，用于指导扩散去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上，MDTE在少数类导向的评估指标上均取得最佳表现，其中少数类召回率提升最高达23.53个百分点，少数类F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点，显著超越最强基线方法。

Conclusion: MDTE通过设计针对性的传播机制与多视角判别融合策略，有效缓解了时序图中少数类被淹没的问题，实现了对少数类更准确、更稳定的识别，为解决类不平衡下的时序图节点分类提供了新范式。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [231] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型预训练中的ELR坍缩现象：学习率（LR）与参数范数通过其比值——有效学习率（ELR）共同主导损失动态。当ELR匹配时，不同学习率和参数范数下的损失轨迹几乎完全重合，跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模均成立。平均坍缩误差仅为约10^-3量级，低于种子间差异。消融实验表明，归一化设计和LR-范数变化的时间尺度是决定坍缩精度的关键因素。干预实验显示，权重衰减和超球形损失函数主要通过诱导的ELR调度影响动态。用ELR替代LR可使拟合的函数缩放律（FSL）在不同范数控制方法间迁移，并解释延迟加速现象。总体上，ELR成为连接学习率调度、范数控制与损失动态的统一坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型预训练中学习率与参数范数对损失动态的影响机制，揭示不同训练配置下损失轨迹的一致性来源，以建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过系统性实验对比不同学习率、参数范数、优化器、架构、数据集和模型规模下的训练过程，分析损失轨迹的收敛性；进行消融实验探究归一化设计与时间尺度的影响；实施控制干预验证权重衰减与超球形损失的作用；构建基于ELR的拟合函数缩放律（FSL）并检验其泛化能力。

Result: ELR匹配时损失轨迹高度一致，平均坍缩误差仅约10^-3，显著低于种子间波动；归一化设计和时间尺度影响坍缩精度；权重衰减与超球形损失通过ELR调度起作用；基于ELR的FSL可在不同范数控制方法间迁移，并解释延迟加速现象。

Conclusion: 有效学习率（ELR）是连接学习率调度、范数控制与损失动态的核心统一坐标，为理解大规模语言模型训练提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [232] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，用于分析基于邻域的公平性审计在有界扰动下的鲁棒性。研究揭示了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化了邻域替换如何传播至审计不稳定性，并引入了审计波动性这一度量指标，用以衡量公平性审计在重复扰动下的期望敏感性。实验结果支持理论分析，表明该框架能解释基准数据集上观察到的审计稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于邻域的公平性审计被广泛使用，但其审计过程本身的鲁棒性尚不清楚。由于这些审计依赖于最近邻关系，特征空间中的微小扰动可能改变局部邻域，从而导致即使模型预测不变，公平性评估结果也不同。因此，亟需一种系统方法来理解并量化这种不稳定性。

Method: 提出一个几何框架，分析在有界扰动下邻居关系的稳定性；推导邻域不变性的充分条件；定义审计波动性作为衡量审计敏感性的指标；通过数学分析和实验验证框架的有效性。

Result: 理论分析与实验结果均表明，所提出的框架能够有效解释基于邻域的公平性审计在实际应用中的稳定性表现，揭示了扰动对审计结果的影响机制，并提供了可量化的鲁棒性评估工具。

Conclusion: 本研究为基于邻域的公平性审计提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了其在特定条件下具有鲁棒性，并提出了新的度量方式（审计波动性）以指导更可靠的公平性评估实践。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [233] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN 是一种多模态空间表示学习框架，通过引入可学习的生物结构归纳偏置来保留生物邻域结构。它在训练时结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性构建生物核函数，提供分级邻域监督并正则化嵌入几何结构，在小鼠大脑和人类肝脏数据上显著优于 BLEEP，且性能提升主要来自生物核正则化而非模型容量增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织学-转录组学方法倾向于强调实例级匹配，忽略了非配对点之间的分子或空间上下文关系，导致生物邻域结构丢失。因此需要一种能显式建模生物空间结构的表示学习框架。

Method: BioKERN 在训练阶段构建一个融合转录组相似性和空间邻近性的生物核函数，利用该核函数提供分级邻域监督，并通过正则化嵌入空间的几何结构来保持生物邻域关系。

Result: 在小鼠大脑 Visium 和人类肝脏 GSE240429 数据集上，BioKERN 在单尺度和多尺度设置下均显著提升了生物邻域检索性能，且控制实验表明性能提升主要归因于生物核正则化而非模型容量增强。

Conclusion: 显式建模生物几何结构作为可解释的归纳偏置，是多模态空间生物学学习中的有效策略，有助于更准确地保留生物邻域信息。
